### [75] [Language Orthogonalization of Self-Supervised Speech Representations for Cross-lingual Parkinson's Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09499)
*Minu Kim,Eunjung Yeo,Kwanghee Choi,June-Woo Kim*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出"语言正交化"方法，通过对自监督语音模型（S3M）特征做闭式岭回归残差化以剔除语言信息，仅用健康对照语音拟合，从而改善帕金森病（PD）检测的跨语言迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S3M 为 PD 检测提供强表征，对缺乏标注患者语音的语言而言，跨语言迁移很有吸引力；但这类表征同时编码了语言身份，会混淆迁移：在无目标语言 PD 语音的情况下，分类器可能区分的是语言而非病理，导致对目标患者高特异度、低敏感度。

Method: 语言正交化：对 S3M 特征针对外部 VoxLingua107 语言嵌入进行闭式岭回归残差化（ridge residualization），仅使用健康对照（HC）语音拟合；在去除可被语言预测的成分同时保留病理相关变异，形成语言依赖性更低的几何结构——HC 表征聚集、PD 表征分散。

Result: 在五种 S3M 骨干、三类语音任务与三种目标语言上，方法一致提升跨语言 PD 检测性能，并纠正了高特异度/低敏感度的失效模式。

Conclusion: 通过以健康语音拟合的语言正交化，可有效解耦 S3M 表征中的语言身份与病理信息，为缺乏目标语言标注数据的 PD 跨语言检测提供了一种简洁有效的特征层面解决方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised speech models (S3Ms) provide powerful representations for Parkinson's disease (PD) detection, making cross-lingual transfer attractive for languages lacking labeled patient speech. However, these representations also encode language identity, which can confound this transfer: without target-language PD speech, classifiers may separate languages rather than pathology, yielding high specificity but low sensitivity on target patients. We propose \emph{language orthogonalization}, a closed-form ridge residualization of S3M features against external VoxLingua107 language embeddings, fitted using only healthy-control (HC) speech. By removing language-predictable components while retaining pathology-related variation, it produces a less language-dependent geometry in which HC representations concentrate while PD representations disperse. Across five S3M backbones, three speech tasks, and three target languages, our method consistently improves cross-lingual PD-detection performance while correcting the high-specificity/low-sensitivity failure.

</details>


### [76] [UniStream: Multi-Expert Residual Vector Quantization for 48 kHz Causal Streaming Audio Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09866)
*Mingyu Zhao,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: UniStream 是面向流式语音、音乐与环境音的全因果 48 kHz 神经音频编解码器，核心为多专家残差向量量化（ME-RVQ）与 OT-CFM 辅助训练目标，支持 12 kbps 与 22.5 kbps 两种码率并实现实时 GPU 推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对流式通用音频编码中量化容量受限的问题，在保持因果、低码率与实时性的前提下提升多类型音频（语音、音乐、环境音）的编码质量，并弥补窄带语音指标评估的不足。

Method: 提出 ME-RVQ，将每层残差量化中单一共享码本替换为四个专家码本，由确定性 Top-K 路由器控制；路由决策仅依赖已解码的量化状态，故解码端可复现所选专家而无需传输专家标识，仅增加 5.5M 参数。同时引入辅助 OT-CFM 目标正则化量化隐空间，该流模块在推理时完全移除，无运行时开销。整体为因果 48 kHz 编码器-解码器框架，支持 12 kbps Top-1 与 22.5 kbps Top-2 模式。

Result: 在 12 kbps 下，UniStream-Top1 的 PESQ 与 UTMOS 与 EnCodec 相当，语音 Mel-D 从 13.07 降至 8.21；在 22.5 kbps 下，UniStream-Top2 取得 ViSQOL 语音模式 4.67、环境音频模式 3.96，后者超过所有 12 kbps 及以下的对比系统，并在 ViSQOL 音频模式下语音任务中距 24 kbps Opus 仅差 0.03 MOS-LQO。消融表明 ME-RVQ 是质量提升的主要来源，OT-CFM 在语音上带来感知增益但有轻微频谱失真代价。

Conclusion: UniStream 通过 ME-RVQ 与 OT-CFM 在因果 48 kHz 框架下实现了高压缩、多类型音频的高质量流式编码，兼顾低码率与实时推理，并提供了 48 kHz 的多维评测基准。

Abstract: We present UniStream, a fully causal 48 kHz neural audio codec for streaming speech, music, and environmental sounds. At its core is Multi-Expert Residual Vector Quantization (ME-RVQ), which replaces the single shared codebook in each residual quantization layer with four expert codebooks controlled by a deterministic Top-K router. Because routing decisions are derived solely from previously decoded quantized states, the decoder can reproduce the selected experts without transmitting expert identifiers, thereby expanding quantization capacity while adding 5.5M parameters. We further introduce an auxiliary Optimal Transport Conditional Flow Matching (OT-CFM) objective to regularize the quantized latent space during training. The flow module is removed entirely at inference and therefore incurs no runtime overhead. UniStream supports a 12 kbps Top-1 mode and a 22.5 kbps Top-2 mode within a causal 48 kHz encoder-decoder framework, while achieving real-time GPU inference. To complement narrow-band speech metrics, we report 48 kHz ViSQOL audio mode, ViSQOL speech mode, standard VGGish-FAD, DNSMOS P.835, and higher-rate reference comparisons with Opus and EnCodec. At 12 kbps, UniStream-Top1 achieves PESQ and UTMOS scores comparable to EnCodec while reducing speech Mel-D from 13.07 to 8.21. At 22.5 kbps, UniStream-Top2 achieves a ViSQOL speech-mode score of 4.67 and an environmental audio-mode score of 3.96, exceeding all evaluated systems operating at 12 kbps or below in the latter setting. It also comes within 0.03 MOS-LQO of Opus at 24 kbps on speech in ViSQOL audio mode. Ablation studies confirm that ME-RVQ is the primary source of quality improvement, whereas OT-CFM provides perceptual gains on speech with a mild trade-off in spectral distortion.

</details>


### [77] [SphereVAE: Hyperspherical Latent Autoencoders for Robust Autoregressive Speech Representation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09903)
*Haoyu Zhang,Jingbin Hu,Hanke Xie,Qirui Zhan,Wenhao Li,Ziyu Zhang,Xiaming Ren,Yue Li,Xunyu Zhu,Zhipeng Chen,Lei Xie*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出SphereVAE，将VAE隐空间约束到单位超球面并采用Power Spherical后验与均匀先验，为自回归预测提供有界几何目标，缓解潜变量漂移，在零样本TTS与长文本生成中降低内容错误率并提升长距离说话人一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散编解码器表征因量化瓶颈，在细粒度韵律、音色、发音及帧间连续性上存在信息缺口；连续表征（如VAE潜变量）虽消除该限制、更适合自回归建模，但其预测误差会沿生成链累积，导致潜变量漂移并损害长文本稳定性。

Method: SphereVAE将VAE隐空间约束到单位超球面上，定义超球面上的Power Spherical后验分布，并将潜变量分布正则化至均匀先验，使信息主要由方向变化编码，从而为自回归预测提供有界几何目标，降低范数漂移风险。

Result: 由于隐空间自由度降低，SphereVAE在重建指标上弱于标准VAE；但集成到VoxCPM用于零样本TTS和长文本生成时，可获得更低的内容错误率，说话人相似度相当，且展现出更稳定的长距离说话人一致性。

Conclusion: 适当的潜空间几何约束能有效缓解语音生成中自回归误差累积与漂移问题。

Abstract: With the rapid development of speech generation technology, discrete codec representations have been widely used because they provide a stable prediction paradigm. In expressive speech generation, however, the quantization bottleneck of discrete codecs results in information gaps in fine-grained prosody, timbre, pronunciation, and frame-to-frame continuity. Continuous representations (e.g., VAE latents), by eliminating this constraint, have emerged as a more effective alternative for autoregressive modeling. Yet when continuous representations are used as autoregressive prediction targets, prediction errors can accumulate along the generation chain, causing latent drift and degrading long-form stability. To mitigate this problem, we propose SphereVAE, which constrains the VAE latent space to the unit hypersphere. SphereVAE defines a Power Spherical posterior on the hypersphere and regularizes the latent distribution toward a uniform prior, so that information is encoded mainly by directional variation, providing a bounded geometric target for autoregressive prediction and reducing the risk of norm drift. SphereVAE underperforms the standard VAE on reconstruction metrics due to reduced latent freedom. However, when integrated into VoxCPM for zero-shot TTS and long-text generation, it yields lower content error rates with comparable speaker similarity, and shows more stable long-range speaker consistency. These results indicate that an appropriate latent geometric constraint can effectively mitigate autoregressive error accumulation and drift in speech generation.

</details>


### [78] [Source-Adaptive Data Curation for Bilingual NVV-Aware ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09929)
*Yuang Cao,Qirui Zhan,Jingbin Hu,Ziyu Zhang,Yunxiang Chen,Houdun Liu,Shuo Feng,Bengu Wu,Lei Xie,Liumeng Xue*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一个中英双语系统，通过NVV-Aware Whisper与源自适应数据策展策略，实现词汇内容与16类非言语发声（NVV）的联合转写，官方评测得分从33.32提升至53.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动语音识别系统通常丢弃笑声、叹息、呼吸、咳嗽等非言语发声，但这些NVV承载着情感与交互信息。ISCSLP 2026 NVVSpeech挑战赛Track 1要求在转写中按相对位置联合标注词汇内容与16类NVV，因此需要一种能同时解码词汇与NVV标签的系统。

Method: 提出NVV-Aware Whisper，基于Whisper-medium，通过检查点兼容的词表重映射，在不扩展词表的前提下将词汇token与内联NVV标签统一到同一自回归序列中解码。同时引入源自适应数据策展策略：一方面通过声学增强与多模态大模型过滤精炼公开NVV语料，另一方面通过自动化预处理与标注从真实媒体中挖掘自发NVV。

Result: 在官方双语评测协议下，最终得分由33.32提升至53.61；消融实验证实各数据策展组件具有互补增益。

Conclusion: 检查点兼容的词表重映射可使Whisper在不扩展词表的情况下联合解码词汇与NVV标签，配合源自适应数据策展策略能够显著提升双语NVV联合转写性能。

Abstract: Nonverbal vocalizations (NVVs), such as laughter, sighs, breaths, and coughs, convey affective and interactional information that conventional automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often discard. We present a bilingual Mandarin-English system for Track 1 of the NVVSpeech Challenge at ISCSLP 2026, which requires joint transcription of lexical content and 16 NVV categories at their transcript-relative positions. Our NVV-Aware Whisper adapts Whisper-medium through checkpoint-compatible vocabulary remapping, enabling lexical tokens and inline NVV tags to be decoded within a unified autoregressive sequence without expanding the vocabulary. To provide reliable and diverse supervision, we further introduce a source-adaptive data curation strategy that refines public NVV corpora through acoustic augmentation and multimodal LLM filtering, while mining spontaneous NVVs from in-the-wild media through automated preprocessing and annotation. Under the official bilingual evaluation protocol, the proposed system improves final score from 33.32 to 53.61, with ablations confirming the complementary benefits of the proposed data-curation components.

</details>


### [79] [NVV-Locator: From Transcript Tags to Acoustic Boundaries for Fine-Grained Nonverbal Vocalization Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09940)
*Yuang Cao,Bingshen Mu,Zhennan Lin,Guojian Li,Haoyue Zhan,Jie Liu,Chuan Xie,Qiang Zhang,Liumeng Xue,Lei Xie*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出 NVV-Locator，用于细粒度非言语发声（NVVs）时间定位，并构建 NVV-TimeBench 基准；模型采用非自回归槽填充架构联合预测词汇时间戳、NVV 类别与事件边界，在基准上取得 71.0% Micro F1、70.2% Macro F1、80.4% Macro mIoU 和 59.6 ms Macro mMAE，优于所评估的大型音频模型，并在外部语料上展现跨语料泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类语音中的非言语发声（如笑声、叹息、呼吸、咳嗽）承载情感与交互信息，但现有方法通常将其表示为转写级标签，缺乏对波形时间边界的细粒度监督。

Method: 1）统一公开资源中的 26 类 NVV；2）通过双 LLM 验证、转写引导强制对齐与基于能量的边界精修构建大规模时间戳监督数据；3）提出专家精修基准 NVV-TimeBench（667 条话语、1,094 个事件）；4）设计非自回归槽填充架构的 NVV-Locator，联合预测词汇时间戳、NVV 类别和事件边界。

Result: 在 NVV-TimeBench 上达到 71.0% Micro F1、70.2% Macro F1、80.4% Macro mIoU、59.6 ms Macro mMAE，优于所评估的大型音频模型；在外部语料上的评估进一步证明其跨语料泛化能力。

Conclusion: NVV-Locator 实现了细粒度 NVV 时间定位，并通过统一类别体系、大规模时间戳监督数据和专家基准，提升了 NVV 边界预测性能与跨语料泛化能力。

Abstract: Human speech includes nonverbal vocalizations (NVVs), such as laughter, sighs, breaths, and coughs, which convey affective and interactional information. Existing approaches typically represent NVVs as transcript-level tags, providing limited supervision for their waveform-time boundaries. We present NVV-Locator for fine-grained NVV temporal grounding. We first unify 26 NVV categories across public resources and construct large-scale timestamp-supervised training data through dual-LLM verification, transcript-guided forced alignment, and energy-based boundary refinement. We further introduce NVV-TimeBench, an expert-refined benchmark with 667 utterances and 1,094 events. NVV-Locator uses a non-autoregressive slot-filling architecture to jointly predict lexical timestamps, NVV categories, and event boundaries. On NVV-TimeBench, it achieves 71.0% Micro F1, 70.2% Macro F1, 80.4% Macro mIoU, and 59.6 ms Macro mMAE, outperforming the evaluated large audio model counterparts. Evaluation on an external corpus further demonstrates the cross-corpus generalization of NVV-Locator.

</details>


### [80] [SpeechAnnotator: A Context-Aware Multi-Agent Framework and Benchmark for Multidimensional Speech Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09947)
*Qirui Zhan,Shuiyuan Wang,Jingbin Hu,Haoyu Zhang,Xiaming Ren,Jinrui Liang,Chaoren Yu,Bengu Wu,Yunxiang Chen,Houdun Liu,Su Feng,Liumeng Xue,Lei Xie*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出SpeechAnnotator：一个本地可部署、上下文感知的多智能体语音标注框架，并配套构建SA-Bench基准与SA-Eval评测体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控语音生成需要带细粒度标注的训练数据（说话人特质、韵律、情感、副语言线索、声学场景、上下文等）。现有标注流程依赖人工校正、付费托管多模态服务或固定处理链，导致标注成本高、依赖外部服务、跨阶段恢复能力弱，难以实现大规模数据处理。

Method: 全基于开源模型与工具构建的本地可部署、上下文感知多智能体框架。前端模块获取说话人感知的语音片段与最终转写；先验证据提取器附加异构片段级线索。三个专门智能体通过共享状态协作：规划智能体将局部音频证据、说话人历史、相邻片段与录音级上下文转化为字段特定契约；标注智能体进行契约引导的多模态预测；评审智能体执行有界评审循环，核查证据支持与跨片段一致性，仅对无支持或不一致字段触发重标注。

Result: 提出SA-Bench，含8.87小时人工标注音频、覆盖九种来源格式；提出SA-Eval，分为Timeline-Eval（说话人感知时间线恢复）、Closed-Eval（有限集属性）、Open-Eval（开放式属性）。实验与消融表明，SpeechAnnotator可作为商用音频系统的本地可部署替代方案，且有界评审循环通过证据与上下文感知的字段级恢复提升多维标注质量。

Conclusion: SpeechAnnotator以开源、本地部署的多智能体协作与有界评审机制，缓解了标注成本、外部服务依赖与跨阶段恢复弱的问题，并借助SA-Bench与SA-Eval弥补评测资源碎片化，提升多维语音标注的表现。

Abstract: Recent controllable speech generation requires training data with fine-grained annotations of speaker traits, prosody, emotion, paralinguistic cues, acoustic scenes, and context. Existing workflows often rely on manual correction, paid hosted multimodal services, or fixed processing chains, which limits large-scale data processing through annotation cost, external-service dependence, or weak cross-stage recovery. We introduce SpeechAnnotator, a locally deployable, context-aware multi-agent framework built entirely from open-source models and tools. Supporting frontend modules first obtain speaker-aware segments and final segment transcripts, while prior evidence extractors attach heterogeneous segment-level cues. Three specialist agents then collaborate through shared state: the Planning Agent converts local audio evidence, speaker history, neighboring segments, and recording-level context into field-specific contracts; the Labeling Agent performs contract-guided multimodal prediction for directly observable attributes; and the Review Agent runs a bounded review loop that checks evidence support and cross-segment consistency, triggering relabeling only for unsupported or inconsistent fields. To address the fragmentation of existing evaluation resources across isolated tasks and narrow-domain test sets, we introduce SpeechAnnotator-Bench (SA-Bench), containing 8.87 hours of human-annotated audio across nine source formats, together with SpeechAnnotator-Eval (SA-Eval), which separates Timeline-Eval for speaker-aware timeline recovery, Closed-Eval for finite-set attributes, and Open-Eval for open-ended attributes. Experiments and ablations show that SpeechAnnotator provides a locally deployable alternative to commercial audio-capable systems, while the bounded review loop improves multidimensional annotation through evidence- and context-aware field-level recovery.

</details>


### [81] [Over-Tightening-Aware Pseudo-Labeling for Tight-Boundary Speaker Diarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09965)
*Shota Horiguchi,Takanori Ashihara,Marc Delcroix,Naohiro Tawara,Alexis Plaquet*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 针对基于因果/反因果模型平均输出生成伪标签导致的"过度收紧"问题，本文分析其成因并提出三项改进：去除停顿填充而非边界填充、引入预热阶段缓解起始处漏检、以及让伪标签协同训练感知最终推理所用的非因果模型。实验表明该方法减少漏检，提升说话人日志与多人语音识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练说话人日志模型时若使用带边界填充或填充停顿的松散标签，模型输出同样松散。为获得更紧的边界，已有工作采用因果与反因果模型平均输出的伪标签方法，但这类伪标签基于估计，易出现过度收紧，导致漏检增多，并以不可恢复的错误传播至下游任务。因此需要厘清过度收紧的成因并加以缓解。

Method: 本文首先分析过度收紧的产生原因，进而提出三种应对策略：(i) 去除停顿填充而非采用边界填充；(ii) 引入预热（burn-in）阶段，缓解因果与反因果预测起始附近的漏检；(iii) 使基于伪标签的协同训练感知用于最终推理的非因果模型。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提方法减少了由过度收紧引起的漏检，同时提升了说话人日志的准确率以及下游多说话人自动语音识别（multi-talker ASR）的性能。

Conclusion: 通过针对过度收紧成因的三项针对性改进，可在保持紧致边界的同时抑制漏检，从而在日志精度与下游多人ASR任务上取得双重收益。

Abstract: Training speaker diarization models on loose labels, such as speech segments with padded boundaries or filled pauses, often results in similarly loose model outputs. To obtain tighter boundaries, pseudo-labeling based on the averaged outputs of causal and anticausal models has been proposed. However, since the pseudo-labels are estimation-based, they can suffer from over-tightening, which increases missed detections that can propagate as unrecoverable errors to downstream tasks. This paper carefully analyzes the causes of over-tightening and proposes three approaches to address them: (i) removing pause filling rather than padding, (ii) introducing a burn-in phase to mitigate missed detections near the beginning of causal and anticausal predictions, and (iii) making pseudo-label-based co-training aware of the non-causal model used for final inference. Experimental results show that the proposed method reduces missed detections caused by over-tightening and improves both diarization accuracy and downstream multi-talker ASR performance.

</details>


### [82] [SCNet: Enhancing GAN-based Speech Generation with Subband Condition Network and Magnitude-aware Phase Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10025)
*Nan Xu,Mingxue Yang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出SCNet，一种在GAN声码器中引入子带条件网络的语音生成方法，通过子带先验信号与傅里叶系数增强重建，并设计幅度感知相位损失以缓解相位缠绕，在客观与主观评测中均取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GAN的声码器虽能从梅尔频谱合成高质量波形，但多以黑盒方式工作，导致频谱中固有的信息丢失，缺乏可解释性与先验约束。

Method: SCNet以轻量级条件网络预测子带信号作为先验知识；该子带信号经STFT变换得到傅里叶系数，并融入主干网络以增强重建；同时提出幅度感知相位损失，用对应幅度对瞬时相位误差加权，突出高能量区域，从而缓解相位缠绕问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，SCNet在高质量语音生成的客观评测与主观评测中均取得优越性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入子带条件先验与幅度感知相位损失，可在GAN声码器框架下有效保留并利用频谱信息，提升语音生成质量。

Abstract: Recent speech generation has been predominantly driven by GAN-based networks aimed at high-quality waveform synthesis from mel-spectrograms. However, these methods often operate as black-box models, leading to the loss of inherent spectral information. In this work, we propose SCNet, a GAN-based vocoder augmented with a Subband Condition Network to address this issue. Specifically, SCNet leverages a subband signal predicted by a lightweight condition network as prior knowledge. This subband signal is then transformed via STFT to obtain Fourier coefficients, which are integrated into the backbone for the enhanced reconstruction. Additionally, to mitigate the phase wrapping, we introduce a magnitude-aware phase loss that computes instantaneous phase errors weighted by the corresponding magnitude, emphasizing regions with higher energy. Experimental results demonstrate that SCNet achieves superior performance in both objective and subjective evaluations for high-quality speech generation.

</details>


### [83] [Pushing the Boundaries of Streaming Multi-Speaker ASR: A Systematic Study of Architectural Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10265)
*Taejin Park,Ivan Medennikov,Kunal Dhawan,Weiqing Wang,Jagadeesh Balam,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个统一框架，将流式多说话人ASR按说话人分离与ASR的集成方式分为四类架构策略，并基于共享的流式ASR与说话人分离基础模型构建四个系统进行系统评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式多说话人ASR需在准确率、延迟、效率之间权衡，同时处理重叠语音并在在线场景下保持长上下文建模；现有研究缺乏对其架构设计空间的系统梳理与部署选择指导。

Method: 以一对开源流式ASR与说话人分离模型为共同基础，依据是否采用多模型实例、是否微调（及其组合），推导出四种多说话人ASR系统，并围绕多说话人准确率、单说话人准确率退化、内存占用、训练复杂度进行对比评测。

Result: 通过系统性的架构分析，明确了流式多说话人ASR的设计空间，并在多说话人准确率、单说话人性能退化、内存与训练复杂度等维度上给出四个系统的权衡表现。

Conclusion: 该工作为在多样化部署约束下选择最合适的流式多说话人ASR方案提供了实用指导与架构层面的清晰认识。

Abstract: Streaming multi-speaker ASR is a challenging task that must balance accuracy, latency, and efficiency while handling overlapping speech and maintaining coherent long-context modeling over extended conversations in an online fashion. We present a unified framework that categorizes streaming multi-speaker ASR into four architectural strategies based on how diarization and ASR are integrated. Using a shared pair of open-source streaming ASR and diarization models as a common foundation, we derive four multi-speaker ASR systems that differ in whether they employ multiple model instances, fine-tuning, or both. We evaluate these systems across multi-speaker accuracy, single-speaker accuracy degradation, memory footprint, and training complexity. Through this systematic architectural analysis, we clarify the design space for streaming multi-speaker ASR and provide practical guidance for selecting the most suitable approach under diverse deployment constraints.

</details>


### [84] [AVSRBench: A Multi-Condition AVSR Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10366)
*Rishabh Jain,Naomi Harte*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 当前AVSR在LRS3上WER低于1%，但该结果受广播语音域限制，难以反映真实泛化能力。本文跨6种条件评测3种AVSR架构，揭示显著泛化鸿沟，并提出RoomReader-AV基准与统一预处理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AVSR在标准LRS3基准上词错误率已低于1%，但该基准依赖广播语音，无法判断性能究竟源于真实泛化能力还是仅域适应。作者希望系统性考察这一差距。

Method: 在六种条件下评测三种AVSR架构：受控广播语音、固定语法话语、超发音Lombard语音、专业与非专业唇读者的朗读语音、以及多人自发对话视频。分析视觉模态性能、音视频融合效果、视角鲁棒性、发音清晰度与相机位移的影响，以及LLM类架构的域外泛化。

Result: 视觉单模态性能在广播域之外迅速退化；音视频融合主要在Lombard语音环境带来收益；90°侧脸视角下视觉理解急剧下降，多模态系统主要依赖声学回退；说话人发音清晰度比轻微相机位移更关键；基于LLM的架构域外泛化较差。

Conclusion: 当前AVSR研究存在显著泛化鸿沟。作者发布RoomReader-AV新基准与统一数据预处理流程，以支持可及的多条件综合评测。

Abstract: While AVSR has achieved sub-1% word error rates on the standard LRS3 benchmark, its reliance on broadcast speech obscures whether this reflects true generalization or just domain adaptation. To investigate this gap, we evaluate three AVSR architectures across six conditions: controlled broadcast speech, fixed-grammar utterances, hyper-articulated Lombard speech, read speech from professional lipspeakers and non-professional speakers, and spontaneous multi-party video conversations. We find that visual-only performance deteriorates rapidly beyond broadcast domains, and audio-video fusion mainly benefits Lombard speech environments. Visual understanding degrades sharply at 90° profile views, with multimodal systems relying largely on acoustic fallback. Additionally, speaker articulation proves more critical than minor camera shifts, and LLM-based architectures suffer from poor out-of-domain generalization. Our work highlights a significant generalization gap in current AVSR research. To address this, we also introduce RoomReader-AV as a new benchmark for AVSR and release a unified data preprocessing pipeline to make comprehensive multi-condition evaluation accessible.

</details>


### [85] [Teacher-Free Self-Distilled Consistency Trajectory Learning for Fast Speech Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10392)
*Shuubham Ojha,Carol Espy-Wilson*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出一种无需外部教师的自我蒸馏一致性轨迹框架用于语音增强，用学生模型的EMA副本生成轨迹目标，配合三阶段课程训练，在VoiceBank+DEMAND上取得宽带PESQ 3.01、ESTOI 0.87、SI-SDR 19.07 dB，并分析了步数与推理调度对感知质量和信号保真度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Schrödinger桥（SB）的一致性轨迹增强器（SBCTMs）虽能实现快速高质量语音增强，但仍需预训练教师模型提供轨迹监督，导致训练成本高，且最终性能受限于教师模型质量。因此需要一种无需外部教师的一致性轨迹框架。

Method: 提出免教师、自蒸馏的一致性轨迹框架：轨迹目标由学生模型的EMA副本生成，而非外部教师；训练采用三阶段课程，包括x0预测、自蒸馏shortcut目标以及使用多分辨率短时傅里叶变换（MR-STFT）损失的感知微调；主干网络与SBCTM相同，为NCSN++。

Result: 在VoiceBank+DEMAND上，无需教师即达到宽带PESQ 3.01、ESTOI 0.87、SI-SDR 19.07 dB。通过变化反向步数和推理调度发现：低反向步数下几何调度最大化感知质量，较高步数下均匀调度更利于信号保真度；几何调度的优势随反向步数增加而缩小。

Conclusion: 该框架在不使用外部教师的情况下实现了与教师依赖方法相当的一致性轨迹语音增强性能，并揭示了推理调度与步数在感知质量与信号保真度之间的权衡规律。

Abstract: Consistency trajectory models offer a route to fast, high-quality speech enhancement, collapsing the many reverse steps of diffusion-based enhancers into a handful. When instantiated on a Schrödinger bridge (SB), which pins the generative process to fixed clean and noisy endpoints, existing consistency-trajectory enhancers (SBCTMs) still require a pretrained teacher to supply trajectory supervision, which raises training cost and ties the final quality to that of the teacher. We propose a teacher-free, self-distilled consistency-trajectory framework that removes the external teacher: trajectory targets are generated by an exponential-moving-average (EMA) copy of the student, and the model is trained with a three-stage curriculum of $\x_0$ prediction, a self-distilled shortcut objective, and perceptual fine-tuning with a multi-resolution short-time Fourier transform (MR-STFT) loss. Using the same NCSN++ backbone as SBCTM, our model attains a wide-band PESQ of $3.01$, ESTOI $0.87$, and SI-SDR $19.07$\,dB on VoiceBank+DEMAND without a teacher. Varying step count and inference schedule we find that a geometric schedule at low reverse step count maximizes perceptual quality, while a higher-step uniform schedule favors signal fidelity, with the geometric advantage narrowing with reverse step count.

</details>


### [86] [Candor-LR: A Dyadic Conversational Dataset for Audio-Visual Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10394)
*Rishabh Jain,Aristeidis Papadopoulos,Zhaofeng Lin,Naomi Harte*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出 Candor-LR，一个源自 CANDOR 语料库（1,656 场自然双人视频会议）的对话式音视频语音识别（AVSR）基准，旨在弥补现有基准（如 LRS3）依赖清晰、脚本化语音的不足，并开源数据处理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AVSR 基准（如 LRS3）主要基于清晰、脚本化且经过排练的语音，无法反映自然对话的复杂性，例如语音重叠、自发轮换发言、非脚本词汇以及多变的声学条件。为推动研究走向真实对话场景，需要一个更贴近实际对话的基准。

Method: 基于 CANDOR 语料库（1,656 场自然双人视频会议）构建对话式基准 Candor-LR。通过自研的数据准备流程，产出训练、验证、测试数据分别为 713.5、10.1 和 60.1 小时，并对预训练 AVSR 模型进行评测，同时考察在干净与含噪条件下的跨域鲁棒性。

Result: 在 Candor-LR 上评测预训练 AVSR 模型发现：相较 LRS3，纯音频准确率大幅下降，但视觉线索能有效补偿，使 Candor-LR 上的性能提升远大于 LRS3；同时在该语料库上训练可显著提升干净与含噪条件下的跨域鲁棒性，因为真实对话数据捕获了更广泛的音视频特征。

Conclusion: Candor-LR 被确立为一个具有挑战性的对话式 AVSR 基准，且作者开源数据处理流程以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Current audio-visual speech recognition (AVSR) benchmarks, like LRS3, rely heavily on clean, scripted and rehearsed speech. They fail to reflect the complexity of natural conversation, which involves overlapping speech, spontaneous turn-taking, unscripted vocabulary and variable acoustic conditions. To shift the field toward realistic dialogue, we introduce Candor-LR, a conversational benchmark derived from the CANDOR corpus of 1,656 natural dyadic videoconferences. Our custom data preparation pipeline yields 713.5, 10.1, and 60.1 hours of training, validation, and test data, respectively. Evaluating pretrained AVSR models on Candor-LR reveals that audio-only accuracy drops sharply compared to LRS3, but visual cues compensate effectively, driving much larger performance gains on Candor-LR than on LRS3. Furthermore, training on this corpus significantly improves cross-domain robustness under both clean and noisy conditions, as its realistic conversational data captures broader audio-video features. We open-source our pipeline to ensure reproducibility, establishing Candor-LR as a challenging benchmark for conversational AVSR.

</details>


### [87] [Phoneme-Aware Pronunciation Representations for L2-English L1-Background Accent Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10466)
*Yangyang Qu,Massimiliano Todisco,Nicholas Evans*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出转录辅助的音素级建模方法，将话语表示为声学片段与对齐音素结合的单位序列，用于L2英语说话人无关口音识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统多采用单一句子级全局表示分类口音，这种全局表示会掩盖依赖特定英语音素的发音线索。

Method: 构建转录辅助模型，使音素信息在口音识别中显式化：用冻结语音编码器提取声学特征，转录仅用于获得音素级强制对齐，将每个语音片段与对齐的英语音素结合为发音单元序列；不向分类器输入词级或句级文本表示。

Result: 在L2-ARCTIC四折说话人无关协议下，达到81.41%准确率和81.21%宏F1，为所评估系统中最高平均性能；消融实验支持音素对齐单元构建的重要性，Whisper消融显示音素信息带来额外增益。

Conclusion: 音素对齐的发音单元序列表示优于全局语音嵌入，转录辅助的音素级建模能有效提升说话人无关的L2英语口音识别性能。

Abstract: We study speaker-disjoint accent identification for L2 English, where the goal is to predict a speaker's first-language (L1) background from English pronunciation. Most existing systems classify accents using a single utterance-level representation, but such global representations can obscure pronunciation cues that depend on specific English phonemes. We propose a transcript-assisted model that makes phoneme information explicit during accent identification. Instead of representing an utterance only as a global speech embedding, we represent it as a sequence of pronunciation units, each combining acoustic evidence from a spoken segment with the aligned English phoneme for that segment. A frozen speech encoder provides the acoustic features, while the transcript is used only to obtain phoneme-level forced alignments. No word-level or sentence-level text representation is passed to the accent classifier. Under a four-fold speaker-disjoint protocol on L2-ARCTIC, our model achieves 81.41% accuracy and 81.21% macro-F1, the highest mean performance among the evaluated systems. Diagnostic ablations support the importance of phoneme-aligned token construction, while a Whisper-based ablation shows an additional gain from phoneme information.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [Round-the-Clock Sub-Ambient Cooling via Dynamic Sky View Factor Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09660)
*Qiuyu Chen,Minghao Dong,Zheng Zhang,Xiaodonog Zhao,Peng Xiao,Zhen Chen*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出受向日葵向光性启发的"动态天空视角因子调控（DSVFS）"系统，通过动态调整辐射发射器角度实现全天候最优辐射制冷，放宽了对发射器光谱选择性的严苛要求，并显著提升冷却功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动日间辐射制冷在能源-水耦合等可持续性问题上具有潜力，但常见的静态水平配置无法响应太阳位置、云量等环境条件的动态变化，因而无法在24小时昼夜循环中优化冷却性能。

Method: 受向日葵向光性启发，开发动态天空视角因子调控（DSVFS）系统：通过动态将发射器转向最优角度，全天候最大化冷却功率，同时放宽对超高热反射率与红外发射率的双重要求；实验采用近黑体发射器与选择性发射器进行验证。

Result: 在典型炎热正午，即使使用近黑体发射器也实现了低于环境温度的冷却；使用选择性发射器时，冷却功率相比静态对照提升135%；全球多城市案例研究表明，年最大节电量可达200 kWh/m²。

Conclusion: 动态调控发射器角度可突破静态辐射制冷的性能限制，降低对材料光谱性能的依赖并大幅提升冷却功率与节能效益，为辐射制冷的实际应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Passive daytime radiative cooling holds significant potential to address sustainability challenges such as the energy-water nexus. However, common static horizontal configurations cannot respond to dynamic changes of environmental conditions such as solar position and cloud coverage, thereby forfeiting the opportunity of optimizing the cooling performance in the 24-h day-night cycle. Here, inspired from the heliotropism of sunflowers, we develop a dynamic sky view factor steering (DSVFS) system. By dynamically steering the emitter to an optimal angle, this system not only relaxes the stringent dual requirement of ultra-high solar reflectivity and infrared emissivity of the radiative emitter, but also maximizes its cooling power round-the clock. Using this DSVFS system, we experimentally demonstrate sub-ambient cooling during a typical hot noon even with a near-blackbody emitter; with a selective emitter, we demonstrate an increase of cooling power by 135% as compared to its static counterpart. Case studies of multiple cities across the world indicate a maximum annual electricity saving of up to 200 kWh/m^2.

</details>


### [89] [A Predictive Design Framework for a Soft Robotic Ventricle using Contractile Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09706)
*Jeongmin Kim,Qiong Wang,Liuyang Cheng,Samuel Tsai,Seong Hyeon Kim,Harma K. Turbendian,Sameh Tawfick*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于集总参数模型的预测性设计框架（AWK3），将软驱动器力学与心脏压力-容积环联系起来，用于设计心室类泵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心血管建模（从多物理场仿真到Windkessel集总参数模型）均未将驱动心室泵的驱动器力学与血流动力学相关联，缺乏从驱动器特性出发预测心脏泵性能的设计工具。

Method: 构建可复现完整循环（含等容期）的体外循环回路；提出相依赖的AWK3（Actuator-driven Windkessel 3-element）集总参数模型，将软驱动器等长特性与心脏P-V环桥接；用由线性收缩串联弹性驱动器或TCPA驱动的心室泵进行验证。

Result: 模型仅凭驱动器等长特性即可同时预测压力与容积（而非传统需固定其一），并能复现Frank-Starling定律，准确描述前负荷、后负荷及收缩性变化下的心脏行为。

Conclusion: 该框架为仿生软体机器人循环系统的设计与高保真控制提供了稳健平台。

Abstract: The natural cardiac cycle is divided into the systole and diastole phases which encompass four distinct stages: isovolumetric contraction and ejection during systole, followed by isovolumetric relaxation and filling during diastole. Cardiovascular modeling of this cycle ranges from high-fidelity multiphysics simulations to reduced-order lumped-parameter (Windkessel) representation of the heart-artery coupling. However, current models do not relate the mechanics of the actuator driving the ventricle pump to the hemodynamics. In this study, we develop and experimentally validate a predictive design framework for ventricle-like pumps using various types of soft contractile actuators. We build a circulatory loop which reproduces the entire loop including the isovolumetric phases-where pressure changes occur without volume shifts. The framework is based on a lumped-parameter model, hereafter referred to as the phase-dependent Actuator-driven Windkessel 3-element (AWK3) model, to bridge soft actuator mechanics to the cardiac pressure-volume (P-V) loop. Unlike traditional models that require either pressure or volume as a fixed input to estimate the other, our proposed model predicts both variables when informed by the isometric characteristics of the actuators. We validate the model using a ventricle-inspired pump driven by a linear contractile series-elastic actuator or twisted and coiled polymer actuators (TCPA). We relate the actuator isometric testing protocol to the phase-dependent AWK3 model, which replicates the Frank-Starling law, accurately describing cardiac behavior under varying conditions of preload, afterload, and inotropy (contractility). This approach provides a robust platform for the design and high-fidelity control of bio-inspired soft robotic circulatory systems.

</details>


### [90] [Maximum signal-to-noise ratio enhancement by averaging under a limited measurement time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09744)
*YingCheng Zhou,Kosuke Minami,Genki Yoshikawa,Gaku Imamura*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了在有限响应时间和固定测量时间下，重复平均并不遵循传统√N规律，而存在严格上界与最优重复次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信号平均理论假设信号幅度与测量时长无关，从而给出√N的SNR增强。但在具有有限响应时间的动态传感系统中该假设不成立，本文旨在厘清统计噪声降低与动态信号衰减之间的竞争关系。

Abstract: Averaging through repetitive measurement is a ubiquitous strategy for improving signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and is commonly assumed to yield a $\sqrt{N}$ enhancement with the number of repetitions $N$. This condition does not generally hold in dynamical sensing systems with finite response time and a fixed measurement time. The enhancement factor is a non-monotonic function of $N$ with a well-defined maximum at an optimal repetition number, beyond which further averaging degrades the SNR. Moreover, below a threshold set by the ratio of measurement time to response time, averaging yields no enhancement at all. These two regimes delimit where the conventional $\sqrt{N}$ law breaks down. Experimental validation using nanomechanical gas sensing, with two receptor-analyte systems deliberately chosen to bracket this enhancement transition, confirms the theoretical predictions. Our results show that measurement time is a finite resource to be optimally partitioned between signal accumulation and averaging, and provide a quantitative guideline for selecting the repetition number in time-constrained sensing such as real-time and repetitive gas or odor detection.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谱滤波主要依据频率或波数幅值选择分量，隐含假设"谱尺度即物理一致性"；但扩散场受算子约束，空间结构与时间衰减存在耦合关系，高波数模态可能仍满足扩散动力学，而低波数模态反而违背控制方程。因此需要一种区分"谱尺度"与"物理一致性"的选谱原则。

Method: 将扩散算子视为在(空间波数 k, 模态衰减率 η)联合空间中的谱几何，物理可行模态位于流形 η=α|k|²。定义算子残差度量各谱分量偏离该流形的程度，并构造有限宽度软投影，按分量到扩散流形的距离而非谱幅值进行筛选；流形宽度控制选择强度。

Result: 数值研究显示，在噪声、扩散率失配和有限采样条件下均能稳健恢复信号；揭示由流形宽度支配的"一致性—保留度"权衡。光热实验进一步证实该投影可抑制离流形谱成分，同时保留主导热响应。

Abstract: Diffusive fields obey operator-imposed relations between spatial structure and temporal decay, yet conventional spectral filtering selects components primarily according to frequency or wavenumber magnitude. Here we show that the diffusion operator defines a spectral geometry in the joint space of spatial wavenumber and modal decay rate, where physically admissible modes occupy the manifold $η=α|\mathbf{k}|^2$. This geometry separates spectral scale from physical consistency: high-wavenumber modes can remain diffusion-consistent, whereas lower-wavenumber modes can violate the governing dynamics. We exploit this distinction by introducing an operator residual and a finite-width soft projection that selects spectral components according to their distance from the diffusion manifold rather than their spectral magnitude. Numerical studies demonstrate robust recovery under noise, diffusivity mismatch, and finite acquisition, and reveal a consistency--retention tradeoff governed by the manifold width. Photothermal experiments further confirm that the projection suppresses off-manifold spectral content while retaining the dominant thermal response. These results establish operator consistency as a spectral-selection principle for diffusive fields and provide a geometric framework for physics-informed processing of dissipative systems.

</details>

*Chenyu Cui,Flavien Bureau,Foucauld Chamming's,Alexandre Aubry,Naiara Korta Martiartu*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建基于矩阵成像的角度畸变矩阵。该矩阵在每个成像点将入射波与反射波沿明确传播方向累积的畸变分离出来，揭示强角度相关性；结合时间反演分析从中估计局部相位像差，作为波速层析成像的可观测量。所获波速图用于改进矩阵成像的传播模型，并通过迭代不断精炼波速，直至残余像差可忽略。

Result: 以超声为概念验证：在组织仿体中得到验证，并在乳腺和肝脏的在体成像中展示临床潜力。声速图显示的对比度与恶性乳腺病灶一致，在健康肝脏中亦符合预期值；像差校正使临床医生常规解读的反射率图像得到锐化。

Conclusion: 该方法能够准确映射波速并校正全场像差，且不局限于超声，可自然推广到任何可实现反射矩阵成像的波模态。

Abstract: Despite being one of the most fundamental properties governing wave propagation, the wave-speed distribution is rarely known accurately in reflection imaging. Its estimation relies on analyzing wave distortions undergone by the incident and reflected waves; however, their contribution is difficult to disentangle from the medium reflectivity, particularly in complex media dominated by speckle. Mismatches between assumed and actual wave speeds result in aberrations that degrade the quality of reflectivity images. Yet these aberrations carry information about the underlying wave-speed heterogeneities. Here, we show that an angular distortion matrix, built upon matrix imaging, can unscramble this information to map the wave speed and correct aberrations across the entire field of view. At each point, this matrix isolates the distortions accumulated by the incident and reflected waves along well-defined propagation directions. It reveals strong angular correlations that can be exploited through time-reversal analysis to estimate local phase aberrations, providing observables for wave-speed tomography. The resulting map improves the propagation model used for matrix imaging, after which the process is iterated to refine the wave speed until residual aberrations become negligible. Using ultrasound as proof of concept, we validate the approach in a tissue-mimicking phantom and illustrate its clinical potential in vivo for breast and liver imaging. Sound-speed maps show contrast consistent with a malignant breast lesion and expected values in a healthy liver, while aberration correction sharpens reflectivity images routinely interpreted by clinicians. Beyond ultrasound, the approach extends naturally to any wave modality in which reflection-matrix imaging can be implemented.

</details>


### [94] [A Multi-Model Non-Intrusive Reduced-Order Framework for Parametric Erosion Prediction via Kinematic Cross-Moment Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09997)

Motivation: 高保真欧拉-拉格朗日模拟弯管颗粒侵蚀每工况耗时数小时，难以快速参数扫描与实时磨损评估；现有ROM锁定单一经验侵蚀公式（如Oka、Finnie），更换材料律或目标硬度即需完全重训练。

Result: 基于90°弯管372个高保真CFD-DPM算例（R/D∈{1.5,2.0,5.0}、5个雷诺数、5个密度比、6个颗粒直径，St>1惯性区），约2 ms即可评估完整二维磨损形貌，主要运动学场R²>0.99；事后评估Finnie完全吻合，Oka、McLaury、Arabnejad近似良好。

Conclusion: 运动学与材料损伤律的解耦使代理模型无需重训练即可支持多经验模型评估，兼顾高精度与毫秒级速度，适用于快速参数扫描与实时磨损评估。

Abstract: High-fidelity Eulerian--Lagrangian simulations of solid particle erosion in curved pipes require hours of compute per operating point, preventing rapid parameter sweeps and real-time wear assessment. Existing reduced-order models (ROMs) speed up these evaluations, yet they are typically trained on a single, fixed empirical erosion formula (e.g., Oka or Finnie). Changing the material law or target hardness then requires a complete retrain of the surrogate. Here, we present a non-intrusive reduced-order framework that avoids this model-locking by approximating the underlying particle collision kinematics instead of scalar wear rates. Specifically, we project and compress 23 Eulerian boundary cross-moments ($\mathbb{E}[V_p^u \sin^vα_p \cos^wα_p]$) across the pipe surface.
  Using 372 high-fidelity CFD-DPM cases of $90^\circ$ elbows over three bend ratios ($R/D \in \{1.5, 2.0, 5.0\}$), five Reynolds numbers, five density ratios, and six particle diameters in the inertial regime ($St > 1$), we evaluate a hybrid compression scheme. Linear Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) and Mode-1 tensor unfolding SVD are combined with block-wise Convolutional Autoencoders (CNN-AE) to handle both broad convective transport and localized impact craters. An anisotropic Gaussian Process Regression (GPR) surrogate maps four dimensionless $Π$-groups to the compressed latent space, evaluating full 2D wear topographies in roughly $2\,\mathrm{ms}$ ($R^2 > 0.99$ on primary kinematic fields). Because kinematics are decoupled from material damage laws, the resulting surrogate evaluates multiple empirical models post-hoc exactly matching Finnie and closely approximating Oka, McLaury, and Arabnejad without retraining.

Result: 推断得到的SPMe以约1 mV量级的误差重现基准电压，并较好地恢复电极容量；正极扩散率在所探测化学计量区间的很大范围内被准确恢复。局部敏感性与Fisher信息分析识别出相关的动力学-欧姆方向与电解液输运方向，并表明在驱动循环大部分区间电压对负极扩散敏感性较弱的情况下，局部化信息与全局扩散率参数化如何产生狭窄的Fisher曲率包络。

Conclusion: 该框架是迈向快速、基于物理的仿真内参数化的一步，可减少对破坏性电芯表征的依赖；同时说明结构冗余去除、参数化方式与敏感性/可辨识性分析对SPMe参数推断的重要性。

Abstract: Physics-based lithium-ion battery models provide access to physically meaningful internal electrochemical states and processes, but cell-specific parameter inference from terminal current-voltage data is computationally expensive and limited by identifiability. We present a surrogate-accelerated inverse framework based on a single-particle model with electrolyte dynamics (SPMe). Its forward map uses our Artiphy surrogate framework for rapid, differentiable evaluation of voltage and selected internal states. After rescaling to remove exact structural redundancies, we infer non-redundant transport, kinetic and capacity parameter groups, including concentration-dependent solid and electrolyte diffusivities. Synthetic voltage data from a Doyle-Fuller-Newman (DFN) model under a WLTP-like current protocol provide a benchmark with known reference parameters and controlled model discrepancy. The inferred SPMe reproduces the benchmark voltage with an error of order 1 mV and recovers electrode capacities well. Positive-electrode diffusivity is recovered accurately over much of the probed stoichiometric range. Local sensitivity and Fisher-information analysis identifies correlated kinetic-Ohmic and electrolyte-transport directions, and shows how localised information and the global diffusivity parameterisation can yield narrow Fisher-curvature envelopes despite weak voltage sensitivity to negative-electrode diffusion over much of the drive cycle. These results represent a step towards rapid physics-based in-silico parameterisation and reduced reliance on destructive cell characterisation.

Abstract: Spectral gaps are widely interpreted as signatures of collective organization, yet it is rarely clear whether the resulting modes correspond to physical variables or merely to convenient mathematical coordinates. Here we show that, in resistor-coupled LC networks, the graph Laplacian defines an experimentally accessible hierarchy of electrical descriptions. Its eigenvectors become measurable voltage patterns, while its eigenvalues quantify the coupling-induced resistive damping of those patterns. When coupling is strong within regions and weak between them, microscopic voltages collapse into nested collective variables. A network of 1002 resonators reduces first to 42 regional voltages and then to four geometry-controlled global modes. Summing the microscopic Kirchhoff equations produces a directly constructible 42-node RLC circuit, without parameter fitting, that reproduces regional dynamics with 1.4 to 4.6% global RMS error. Finer modal coordinates improve accuracy but no longer correspond generally to simple resistor graphs. Robustness tests show that the regional level survives substantial conductance disorder and internal rewiring, but weakens when perturbations impair mixing within a region; changes in interregional connectivity selectively disrupt the global level. Thus the Laplacian spectrum becomes a physical design principle: it identifies which collective electrical variables emerge, how they can be constructed, and when they provide an adequate reduced description.
