  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估AlN保护层对InGaN/GaN多量子阱在高温退火过程中的保护效果，明确高温热处理对量子阱结构与发光动力学的影响，为高温工艺兼容性提供依据。

Method: 对覆盖AlN薄膜的InGaN/GaN多量子阱进行500–1100 °C系列退火；室温325 nm激光激发下测光致发光(PL)光谱；结合PL激发(PLE)、时间分辨PL(TRPL)、功率依赖PL、速率方程建模、色坐标分析及飞行时间二次离子质谱(ToF-SIMS)表征。

Result: PL谱呈现363 nm紫外、455 nm蓝光与565 nm黄光三个发射带；455 nm量子阱发光在1100 °C退火后仍保留；功率依赖PL表明载流子复合机制未受热处理影响；速率方程与色坐标分析显示等效发射寿命与发光颜色受热影响有限；ToF-SIMS进一步证实退火样品中量子阱结构得以保持。

Conclusion: AlN保护层有效维持了InGaN/GaN多量子阱在高温退火下的结构完整性与发光特性，其复合机制与发光寿命具有较好的热稳定性。

Abstract: The thermal stability and carrier recombination kinetics of an as-received InGaN/GaN multiple quantum well (MQW) structure, capped with a protective AlN thin film, are studied following a series of thermal annealings at temperatures between 500 deg C and 1100 deg C. Under 325 nm focused laser excitation at room temperature, the sample's photoluminescence (PL) spectrum exhibits three emission bands: an ultraviolet peak at 363 nm, a blue peak at 455 nm, and a yellow peak at 565 nm. We find that the MQW's 455 nm emission is preserved after annealing at 1100 deg C. Moreover, room-temperature PL excitation (PLE) and time-resolved PL (TRPL) have been investigated to shed light on the sample's energy-transfer mechanisms and emission decay characteristics. Power-dependent PL spectra analysis shows that the carrier recombination mechanism of the MQW's emission has not been affected by thermal treatment. Rate equation modelling and chromaticity coordinate analysis also show limited thermal impact on the calculated equivalent emission lifetime and emissive colour. Time-of-flight secondary ion mass spectrometry (ToF-SIMS) analysis has been performed, further evidencing the preservation of the MQW structure in the annealed sample.

</details>


<div id='nlin.PS'></div>

# nlin.PS [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Fast Synergetic Simulation to Study Slow Evolution of Soliton Patterns in Optical Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10389)
*Sanzida Akter,Pradyoth Shandilya,Logan Courtright,Amir Leshem,Giuseppe D'Aguanno,Omri Gat,Curtis R. Menyuk*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 提出"协同法"数值格式，消除快速阻尼自由度、保留慢变模式，将驱动Kerr腔中孤子分子模拟加速10³–10⁵倍，实现实验室时间尺度上的长时动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理与生物系统中的复杂图案常由少数关键变量主导的缓慢集体动力学产生。非线性光谐振腔中的耗散Kerr孤子是典型例子：孤子间相互作用的时间尺度远长于损耗与增益特征时间。但因系统刚性（stiffness），传统数值方法必须用极小时间步去解析大量快速阻尼自由度，直接模拟代价极高。

Method: 提出协同法（synergetic method）：通过消除快速阻尼自由度、仅保留慢变演化模式，构造能采用比传统方法大若干数量级时间步的数值格式。将其应用于驱动Kerr腔中的孤子分子。

Result: 在驱动Kerr腔孤子分子问题上获得10³至10⁵倍加速，同时准确捕捉实验室时间尺度上的动力学；据此模拟了三孤子分子的完整相互作用动力学以及八孤子分子的演化过程。

Conclusion: 该方法为研究时间尺度分离显著的非线性系统中的缓慢图案形成提供了高效框架。

Abstract: Complex patterns in physical and biological systems often emerge through slow collective dynamics governed by a small number of key variables. In nonlinear optical resonators, dissipative Kerr solitons provide an important example, where interactions between well-separated solitons can evolve over timescales far longer than the characteristic loss and gain timescales. Direct numerical simulation of these dynamics is challenging because stiffness forces conventional methods to resolve many rapidly damped degrees-of-freedom with very small time steps. We present a numerical scheme, the synergetic method, that eliminates these rapidly damped degrees-of-freedom and retains the slowly evolving modes, enabling time steps many orders of magnitude larger than those used in conventional approaches. Applied to soliton molecules in driven Kerr cavities, the method achieves speedups of $10^3$ to $10^5$ while capturing dynamics on laboratory timescales. We use it to model the full interaction dynamics of a three-soliton molecule and the evolution of an eight-soliton molecule. The approach provides an efficient framework for studying slow pattern formation in nonlinear systems with widely separated timescales.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Voice or Stereotype? Disentangling Acoustic and Content-Based Gender in Speech-to-Speech Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09263)
*Xiaoqun Liu,Tanu Mitra,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文通过一项受控实验，考察语音到语音（S2S）模型是否会因文本内容中的性别刻板印象而偏移输出语音的感知性别（语音渲染），以及模型判断说话人性别时依据的是声音还是内容（性别归因）。结果发现语音渲染无刻板漂移，但所有模型均依据内容而非声音判断性别，暴露了固定输出语音评估无法察觉的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S2S模型已应用于配音、翻译与语音代理，它们能听到承载说话人性别的声音。一个忠实的系统应依据说话人听起来像谁，而非谁通常说这些话来对待说话人。然而现有S2S模型多用单一固定输出语音应答，硬编码使其无法漂移向刻板印象，导致输出语音检查即使面对有偏模型也显示正常。因此需要检验偏见是否隐藏在评估盲区。

Method: 设计一项受控实验，将男性和女性声音与男性化、中性、女性化刻板印象的文本段落进行交叉组合，在五种开源与闭源模型上、以英语、西班牙语和普通话三种语言进行测试。提出两个问题：模型复述输入时，词语中的刻板印象是否使输出语音的感知性别发生偏移（语音渲染）？模型陈述说话人性别时，是跟随声音还是内容（性别归因）？

Result: 渲染语音未表现出刻板漂移。但所有模型均根据内容而非声音判断说话人性别。将内容向女性化方向推进一级（男性化→中性→女性化），会使判定为“女性”的几率增加1.7至24倍。当内容与声音冲突时，表现最差的模型在90%的情况下误判说话人性别；当二者一致时，误判率仅为2%。

Conclusion: 偏见隐藏在性别归因环节，而固定输出语音的评估方式无法察觉。随着S2S系统日益为真实人群发声，性别审计必须将注意力投向此处。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech (S2S) models now run inside dubbing, translation, and voice agents. Unlike text models, they hear the speaker's voice, which carries the speaker's gender. A faithful system should treat a speaker as who they sound like, not as whoever usually says what they said. Testing this is harder than it looks, since most S2S models answer in a single, fixed output voice, hard-coded so it cannot drift toward a stereotype. Checking the output voice comes back clean even when the model is biased. We therefore ask two questions. When a model re-speaks the input, does the stereotype in the words shift the perceived gender of the output voice (voice rendering)? And when the model states the speaker's gender, does it follow the voice or the content (gender attribution)? We answer both with one controlled experiment crossing male and female voices with masculine-, neutral-, and feminine-stereotyped passages, on five open- and closed-source models in English, Spanish, and Mandarin. The rendered voice shows no stereotype drift. But every model decides the speaker's gender from the content, not the voice. Making the content one step more feminine (masculine -> neutral -> feminine) multiplies the odds of a "female" judgment by 1.7-24. When the content clashes with the voice, the worst model misgenders the speaker in 90% of cases. When they agree, it misgenders in only 2%. The bias thus hides in gender attribution, where fixed-voice evaluation cannot see, and where audits must look as S2S systems increasingly speak for real people.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Accuracy: ARIA-Rubrics for Evaluating Audio Reasoning in Large Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09681)
*Yupei Li,Qiyang Sun,Mohamed Mady,Chenxi Wang,Zhengwei Gong,Berrak Sisman,Björn Schller*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出ARIA-Rubrics，一个轻量、免标注、可解释的音频推理质量评估框架，通过六个互补指标从感知基础、推理连贯性和答案一致性三个维度评估大音频语言模型的推理过程，实验证明其与人类判断高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LALMs评估仅依赖准确率，难以区分真实推理与表层模式匹配，易高估推理能力；现有过程评估方法依赖昂贵的人工标注或黑箱式的LLM-as-judge，缺乏透明度且有偏；且音频推理特有的感知幻觉与跨模态对齐问题使文本评估框架无法直接迁移。

Method: 提出ARIA-Rubrics（音频推理完整性评估）框架，构建轻量、免标注的黄金推理链，采用Chain-of-Thought提示作为推理过程外部化机制，使推理过程可观测，并用六个互补指标从感知基础、推理连贯性、答案一致性三个方面量化评估音频推理质量。

Result: 在2个基准上对9个模型进行实验，识别出当前LALMs的三种推理模式，并给出可操作的改进方向；ARIA-Rubrics与人类判断达到高相关性。

Conclusion: ARIA-Rubrics提供了一种自动、透明、低成本的音频推理过程评估方案，弥补了准确率评估与黑箱评判的不足，可用于诊断LALMs推理模式并指导其推理能力提升。

Abstract: Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) have shown strong performance on audio reasoning benchmarks, but accuracy alone cannot distinguish true reasoning from superficial pattern matching, often overestimating reasoning ability since high scores may result from guessing rather than genuine audio understanding. Evaluating the reasoning process itself is essential for improving LALMs' reasoning ability, yet remains challenging. Existing methods either rely on costly human annotation or opaque LLM-as-judge approaches, making them impractical, biased, and lacking transparency. Moreover, audio reasoning introduces unique challenges absent in text-based settings, perceptual hallucination and cross-modal alignment between audio understanding and textual inference, hence text-based evaluation frameworks cannot be directly applied. Therefore, we propose ARIA-Rubrics (Audio Reasoning Integrity Assessment), a lightweight, annotation-free gold reasoning chains, automatic and transparent framework comprising six complementary metrics that evaluate audio reasoning quality across perceptual grounding, reasoning coherence, and answer consistency. We use Chain-of-Thought prompting as an externalization mechanism to make the reasoning process observable. Experiments on 9 models across 2 benchmarks identify three reasoning modes of current LALMs with actionable directions for future development, with ARIA-Rubrics achieving high correlation with human judgments. The code is available at the Github Repository.

</details>


### [101] [Arti-JEPA: Adapting Video World Model to Real-Time MRI of the Vocal Tract for Speech-Production Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09757)
*Hong Nguyen,Sean Foley,Christina Hagedorn,Yijing Lu,Sudarsana Reddy Kadiri,Dani Byrd,Shrikanth Narayanan*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出 Arti-JEPA，一种面向实时 MRI 声道视频的联合嵌入预测架构，通过在 62 小时无标注数据上自监督预训练，冻结表征在音素预测、口吃分类与术后迁移三项任务上取得关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rtMRI 能捕捉完整声道动态，但标注数据稀缺，且其单切片、灰度、低分辨率特性与视频基础模型训练所用的自然视频差异巨大，导致通用视频模型难以直接迁移。

Method: 提出 Arti-JEPA 联合嵌入预测架构，在约 62 小时无标注声道视频上延续自监督目标进行域适应，并以冻结表征评估三项任务：跨域音素预测、流利与不流利（口吃）分类，以及部分舌切除术前/术后迁移表征。

Result: （1）时序视频先验显著优于逐帧图像编码器，潜空间预测（V-JEPA）至少与像素重建（VideoMAE）相当，并在细粒度音素上更优；（2）域适应效果依赖任务，跨域音素预测 κ 约翻倍至 0.352，但对二分类口吃任务无增益；（3）Arti-JEPA 能从术前术后语音中恢复音素信号，域内探针解码患者效果不低于典型说话人，表明残余迁移差距源于跨说话人/跨域错位而非手术信号损失，且术后解码不低于术前。

Conclusion: 研究表明，冻结且经域适应的 rtMRI 编码器可作为可复用的测量工具，服务于发音与临床语音科学研究。

Abstract: Real-time MRI (rtMRI) captures the dynamics of the entire vocal tract during speech, but labeled data are scarce and the modality - single-slice, grayscale, low-resolution - differs substantially from the natural videos that video foundation models are trained on. We introduce Arti-JEPA, a joint embedding predictive architecture to model vocal tract rtMRI by continuing its self-supervised objective on about 62h of unlabelled vocal-tract videos, and evaluate the frozen representation on three tasks: cross-domain phoneme prediction (on typical speakers), fluent-vs-disfluent classification (a corpus containing stuttered speech), and characterizing pre/post-operative transfer (after partial glossectomy). Three key findings emerge. (1) A temporal video prior decisively outperforms per-frame image encoders, and latent prediction (V-JEPA) is at least as strong as pixel reconstruction (VideoMAE), with the edge on fine-grained phonemes. (2) Domain adaptation is \emph{task-dependent}: it roughly doubles cross-domain phoneme prediction $κ$ (to 0.352) but does not help binary stuttering classification. (3) Arti-JEPA was able to recover phoneme signal from pre/post glossectomy speech --- an in-domain probe decodes patients at least as well as a typical speaker, indicating that the residual transfer gap is cross-speaker/domain misalignment, not surgical signal loss, and post-operative decoding does not fall below performance on pre-operative speech. Together, these position a frozen, domain-adapted rtMRI encoder as a reusable measurement tool for articulatory and clinical speech science.

</details>


### [102] [Robust Rank Aggregation for Multimodal Speech-Based Alzheimer's Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09948)
*Zemin Jin,Tomoko Matsui*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出秩聚合框架，用归一化预测秩替代后验概率融合语音AD检测的多模态模型，并结合置信门控随机森林，在ADReSS2020/ADReSSo2021上分别达95.83%和90.14%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语音的AD检测借助多模态基础模型整合声学与语言信息，但常规的后验概率平均不可靠：不同分类器的后验概率存在尺度不匹配，相同数值可能代表不同置信度。

Method: 提出稳健的秩聚合框架，聚合归一化预测秩而非后验概率。每个受试者按其在固定训练队列的out-of-fold预测分布中的百分位打分；因秩排序对单调变换不变，可规避概率尺度失配并保留置信度排序。再用置信门控随机森林，基于临床可解释的语言特征纠正残差错误，仅当两者不一致且RF高置信时覆盖秩预测，无需额外深度模型训练或显式后验概率校准。

Result: 在ADReSS2020和ADReSSo2021数据集上准确率分别为95.83%和90.14%，优于既往报道结果。

Conclusion: 秩聚合可有效解决多模态分类器概率尺度不一致问题，配合置信门控随机森林纠错，在语音AD检测中以轻量方式取得有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Speech-based Alzheimer's disease (AD) detection has recently benefited from multimodal foundation-model representations that integrate complementary acoustic and linguistic information. However, conventional probability averaging over these complementary classifiers is unreliable, because their posterior probabilities exhibit mismatched scales: identical values may reflect different confidence levels across models. We propose a robust rank aggregation framework that aggregates normalized prediction ranks instead of posterior probabilities. Each subject is scored by its percentile within a fixed training-cohort distribution of out-of-fold predictions; since rank ordering is invariant to monotonic transformations, this avoids probability-scale mismatch while preserving classifier confidence ordering. A confidence-gated Random Forest further corrects residual errors using clinically interpretable linguistic features, overriding the rank prediction only when the two disagree and the RF is highly confident, without additional deep model training or explicit posterior-probability calibration. On ADReSS2020 and ADReSSo2021, the method achieves accuracies of 95.83% and 90.14%, respectively, comparing favorably with previously reported results.

</details>


### [103] [Deterministic Prompting for Speaker-Stable Low-Resource Greek TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10022)
*Georgios Syllas,Efthymios Georgiou,Kosmas Kritsis,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一套数据策管流程，将有声书录音通过 WhisperX 对齐与筛选转化为 TTS 可训练数据，并对 Parler-TTS（880M）进行微调，实现低资源单体希腊语 TTS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 TTS 在高资源语言上接近人类水平，但在干净语音稀缺时性能下降。希腊语即为典型，缺少支撑 SOTA 合成所需的精选语料。

Method: 提出有声书转 TTS 数据的数据策管配方（WhisperX 对齐+筛选）；微调 Parler-TTS（880M），利用其预训练中的可迁移语音先验；将 LLM 生成风格提示替换为确定性提示以缓解推理时说话人漂移；在 3.5 小时单说话人数据上训练说话人专属 LoRA，仅更新约 5% 参数以锚定身份。

Result: WER 10.7%（高出 ASR 下限 2.9），MOS-I 4.00（人类语音 4.36），说话人一致性接近人类（MOS-C 4.24 vs. 4.30）。

Conclusion: 在有限精选数据下，可构建稳健的单说话人希腊语 TTS。

Abstract: Modern TTS systems approach human quality for high-resource languages but degrade when clean speech data is scarce. Modern Greek exemplifies this, lacking the curated corpora behind state-of-the-art synthesis. We propose a data curation recipe that transforms audiobook recordings into TTS-ready data via WhisperX alignment and filtering. Then we fine-tune Parler-TTS (880M), a prompt-based multilingual model whose pre-training encodes phonetic priors transferable to Greek. During development, we find that LLM-generated style prompts introduce speaker drift at inference. Replacing them with deterministic prompts resolves this, and a speaker-specific LoRA stage trained on 3.5 h of single-speaker data anchors identity while updating ~5% of parameters. Our system achieves WER 10.7% (2.9 above the ASR floor), MOS-I 4.00 (vs. 4.36 human speech), and near-human speaker consistency (MOS-C 4.24 vs. 4.30), showing that robust single-speaker Greek TTS is achievable with limited curated data.

</details>


### [104] [Zero-Shot Temporal Localisation of Audio Deepfakes in Multi-Speaker Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10051)
*Soumyadeep Roy*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出"多说话人对话中的时序深度伪造定位"(TDLMC)任务,指出现有话语级检测器无法报告伪造语音位置,以及EER/min-DCF在该场景下不适定;作者提出免训练的5阶段流水线,包裹冻结的二值检测器,用双阈值滞回有限状态机解码器将噪声窗口分数转为连贯伪造区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音克隆欺诈越来越多采用"外科手术式注入"：在一段真实对话中仅替换一两句话为合成语音。这类攻击下，话语级深度伪造检测器只能对整个音频片段输出单一真/假标签，无法指出合成语音的具体位置，因此需要能定位伪造语音时间区间的检测方法。此外，当同一文件同时包含真假两类语音时，EER和min-DCF等指标本身不适定，需要新的时序评价指标。

Method: 提出免训练的5阶段流水线：包裹一个冻结的现有二值检测器，无需重新训练即可输出片段级结果；核心是双阈值滞回有限状态机解码器，将带噪的滑窗分数转化为连贯的伪造时间区间；并在保留的校准集上选取解码器常量，实现零样本定位。

Result: 在基于ASVspoof 5构建的180段多说话人对话上，使用强骨干网络时达到时序IoU 0.90、时序检出率0.95、MS-DCF 0.26；真实语音上误报率低于6%，在真实多说话人对话AMI语料上低于2%。相同流水线下，训练式定位器仅将时序IoU提升约0.04，说明放弃监督的代价有限。在三个冻结检测器上验证，残余误报主要源于骨干网络的域差距而非解码器。

Conclusion: 该工作首次形式化TDLMC任务并给出时序评价指标，提供首个零样本基线及可复用基准；证明免训练的滞回解码流水线可在不重训检测器的前提下实现高精度伪造语音时序定位，且监督方法的性能增益有限。

Abstract: Voice-cloning fraud increasingly relies on surgical injection: a genuine conversation in which only one or two sentences are replaced by synthetic speech. Utterance-level deepfake detectors emit a single real/fake label per clip and cannot report where the synthetic speech lies. We formalise this as Temporal Deepfake Localisation in Multi-Speaker Conversations (TDLMC), show that equal error rate and min-DCF are ill-posed once a file contains both classes, and propose temporal metrics for this regime. Our contribution is a training-free five-stage pipeline that wraps a frozen binary detector and adds segment-level output with no retraining, using a two-threshold hysteresis finitestate-machine decoder to turn noisy window scores into coherent intervals. On 180 constructed multi-speaker conversations from ASVspoof 5, the system attains temporal intersection-over-union 0.90, temporal detection rate 0.95, and MS-DCF 0.26 with a strong backbone, and its false-alarm rate on genuine speech is below 6%, falling under 2% on genuine real multi-speaker dialogue (AMI). Under an identical pipeline, a trained localiser improves temporal IoU by only about 0.04, bounding the cost of forgoing supervision. Evaluated across three frozen detectors under one decoder whose constants are selected on a held-out calibration split, and with a controlled analysis attributing the residual false-alarm rate to a backbone domain gap rather than to the decoder, this provides the first zero-shot baseline and a reusable benchmark for TDLMC.

</details>


### [105] [Orukeet: Multilingual ASR with Frozen Gabor Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10054)
*Nathan Roll,Irene Yi,Büşra Marşan,Vianney Grenez,Gabriel Stein,Momcilo Mrkaic,Pavle Padjin,Vladimir Zeljkovic,Calbert Graham*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: Orukeet将Parakeet编码器中一半的时间滤波器替换为12,288个拟合Gabor核并冻结，其余参数在多语言多口音数据上训练，在25种语言FLEURS上词错率相对降低10.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持Parakeet原有架构与推理算子的前提下，提升其多语言、多口音语音识别的准确率。

Method: 将适配后的Parakeet编码器中一半时间滤波器替换为12,288个拟合的Gabor核并冻结，训练剩余参数，使用多语言多口音数据；最终适配与检查点选择基于LibriSpeech test-other。

Result: 在25种语言、20,146条FLEURS录音上，汇总WER从11.01%降至9.85%（相对降低10.6%）；25种语言中23种WER更低；74个测试划分中61个优于Parakeet，包括test-clean 1.46% vs 1.53%、test-other 2.86% vs 3.14%、FLEURS英语3.82% vs 4.28%。

Conclusion: 用冻结的拟合Gabor核替换部分时间滤波器可在保留Parakeet架构与推理算子的条件下显著提升多语言识别性能；拟合核以普通卷积权重存储。

Abstract: Orukeet replaces half of an adapted Parakeet encoder's temporal filters with 12,288 fitted Gabor kernels, freezes these replacements, and trains the remaining parameters on multilingual and multi-accent data. Final adaptation and checkpoint selection use LibriSpeech test-other. Across 20,146 FLEURS recordings in 25 languages, pooled word error rate (WER) falls from Parakeet's 11.01% to Orukeet's 9.85%, a 10.6% relative reduction. Orukeet has lower WER on 23 of the 25 languages. Orukeet outperforms Parakeet on 61 out of 74 tested splits, including LibriSpeech test-clean (1.46% vs. 1.53% WER), test-other (2.86% vs. 3.14%), and FLEURS English (3.82% vs. 4.28%). All comparisons decode the same audio with matched NeMo settings. The fitted kernels are stored as ordinary convolution weights, retaining Parakeet's architecture and inference operators.

</details>


### [106] [NOPE-HYPE: A Structured Simulation Workflow for Robust Speech-to-Text Across Diverse Acoustic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10058)
*Niramay M. Patel,Bibek Behera,Raksha Sharma*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出NOPEHYPE训练工作流：结合可控环境模拟器、基于PSD模板的覆盖率最优环境缩减，以及小型可解释的超参数搜索，用于提升语音转文本翻译系统在多样声学条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际语音转文本翻译系统需在多样声学环境下可靠运行，但现有流程缺乏可控工具进行系统化的环境探索；大规模语音模型对未见声学条件仍敏感，根源在于训练数据难以覆盖真实环境的完整范围。

Method: NOPEHYPE包含三部分：(1) 可控环境模拟器；(2) 在功率谱密度（PSD）模板上做覆盖率最优的环境缩减，得到有原则的环境原型集；(3) 针对模拟器旋钮的小规模、可解释超参数搜索（27次运行的结构化扫描）。

Result: 模拟器生成的噪声在Whisper与SeamlessM4T模型上取得与均衡真实噪声训练相当的性能；给出了有原则的环境原型集；并通过结构化27次运行超参数扫描确定了实用的默认模拟器配置。

Conclusion: 可控模拟器可替代或补充真实噪声训练，结合PSD覆盖缩减与轻量超参搜索，为鲁棒语音转文本翻译提供可复现、可解释的环境探索与训练范式。

Abstract: Robust speech-to-text translation systems should perform reliably across diverse acoustic conditions, yet practical pipelines lack controllable tools for systematic environment exploration. Large speech models remain sensitive to unseen acoustic conditions, as training data rarely cover the full range of real environments.We present NOPEHYPE, a structured training workflow that combines a controllable environment simulator, coverage-optimal environment reduction on Power Spectral Density (PSD) templates, and a small, interpretable hyperparameter search over simulator knobs. We show that simulator-generated noise achieves performance comparable to balanced realnoise training across Whisper and SeamlessM4T models, provide principled environment prototype sets, and identify practical default simulator configurations from a structured 27-run hyperparameter sweep.

</details>


### [107] [TimeCues Studio: A Workspace for Music Annotation and Algorithm Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10338)
*Sapir Caduri,Yoav Goldberg*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: TimeCues Studio 是一个面向团队的开源音乐标注与算法开发一体化工作台，支持在锁定网格的时间轴上放置多种带歧义感知的标记，集成算法对比引擎、Python 原型沙箱与歧义感知评估器，MIT 许可并通过单条 Docker Compose 命令部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多媒体应用需要精确的音乐标注（如位置、片段或循环），人工或算法均可完成。机器学习算法虽可扩展且有效，但依赖标注训练数据，而许多任务缺乏此类数据；同时现有工具多为单曲标注设计，难以支持团队对整批语料库的标注，且与算法开发过程脱节。

Method: TimeCues Studio 构建一个开源工作空间：标注者在一个对齐网格的时间轴上放置多种标记类型，每种标记支持歧义感知标注；时间轴可视化多种音乐特征（含分离的音轨/stems）。同一时间轴驱动算法对比引擎（包含内置基线）、用于原型新模型的 Python 沙箱，以及尊重结构化字段的歧义感知评估器。系统以 MIT 许可发布，通过单条 Docker Compose 命令部署。

Result: 该工具面向团队批量标注音乐语料库并与算法开发紧密集成，突破了现有工具单曲标注的局限；其时间轴同时适用于音乐同步项目中的单人标注者。内置基线、原型沙箱与歧义感知评估器形成了标注—对比—原型—评估的闭环。

Conclusion: TimeCues Studio 为算法开发团队提供集标注、算法对比与原型验证于一体的开源工作空间，具备歧义感知能力与便捷部署方式，可支持规模化音乐语料库标注及音乐同步等应用场景。

Abstract: Multimedia applications require precise music annotation-labeled positions, segments, or loops-placed by hand or algorithmically. Machine-learning algorithms are scalable and effective but need annotated training data, scarce for many tasks. TimeCues Studio is an open-source workspace where algorithm-development teams annotate a music corpus, compare detection algorithms against those annotations, and prototype new ones. Unlike existing tools built for a single track at a time, TimeCues targets teams annotating whole collections, tightly integrated with algorithm development. Annotators place several marker types-each supporting ambiguity-aware labeling-on a grid-locked timeline that visualizes many music features, including separated audio stems. The same timeline drives an algorithm-comparison engine with bundled baselines, a Python sandbox for prototyping new models, and an ambiguity-aware evaluator that honors the structured fields. The same visualization suits solo annotators on music-sync projects. TimeCues is MIT-licensed and deploys via one Docker Compose command.

</details>


### [108] [Unifying Score and Performance for Fine-Grained Music Understanding in Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10351)
*Milan Liessens Dujardin,Song-Ze Yu,Kevin Miao*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出MuNo-SP（统一乐谱与演奏的文本表示）及其数据生成流程，构建了含148篇长篇演奏分析与31,080组问答对的古典钢琴数据集MAESTROCaps，用于提升大型音频语言模型对音乐演奏细节的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频语言模型训练依赖粗糙、弱对齐的文本描述，难以支撑音乐中力度、分句、 articulation、时间等演奏细节的学习，限制了其在教育与艺术实践中的应用。

Method: 提出MuNo-SP文本表示，联合编码乐谱内容与演奏信息；基于该表示，利用对齐的乐谱-演奏数据自动生成长篇听觉分析与音乐领域问答对，并据此构建MAESTROCaps数据集。

Result: MAESTROCaps包含148篇长篇演奏分析和31,080组问答对，源自148对对齐的乐谱-演奏数据；人工评测中，MuNo-SP分析在9个片段中的8个以多数票优于纯MIDI分析；在乐谱-演奏理解基准上也表现强劲。

Conclusion: 融合乐谱与演奏信息可为大型音频语言模型提供比纯MIDI基线更可靠、更具音乐信息量的监督信号。

Abstract: Large audio language models (LALMs) have shown promising progress in broad music-understanding tasks such as tagging, retrieval, and captioning. Music understanding that requires finer hearing over both the content and how it is realized within a performance through dynamics, phrasing, articulation, time, and other performance techniques, however, remains at an earlier stage. Existing audio-language model (ALM) training pipelines typically rely on coarse, weakly grounded captions and therefore provide little support for learning these subtle nuances in music, limiting their ability to serve real-world applications in education or artistic practice.
  We therefore introduce MuNo-SP (Music Notation unifying Score and Performance), a text-based representation that jointly encodes score content and performance information. Building on MuNo-SP, we develop an automatic training-data generation pipeline that uses aligned scores and performances to produce long-form auditory analyses and musically informed question-answer pairs. We use this pipeline to construct MAESTROCaps, a classical piano dataset comprising 148 long-form performance analyses and 31,080 question-answer pairs derived from 148 aligned score-performance pairs. In a human evaluation, MuNo-SP analyses were preferred by majority vote over MIDI-only analyses for eight of nine excerpts. MuNo-SP also performed strongly on a benchmark of score-performance understanding, suggesting that integrating score and performance information enables more reliable and musically informative LALM supervision than a MIDI-only baseline.

</details>


### [109] [Vocal Music under Phoneme-Conditional Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30823)
*Hayoon Kim,Kyogu Lee*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出"音位条件分析"方法，隔离类型学上具区分性音位的声学效应，在九种语言、数千首歌曲中沿五个声学维度测量，实现无伴奏人声语言识别85.5%的平衡准确率，表明音系结构在歌唱方式中留下系统可测的痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每种语言的声乐即使无器乐伴奏也带有独特的听觉身份，但这一差异是否可测量、能否追溯到具体音位尚不清楚。作者希望回答语言间歌唱音色差异的可量化性及其音位来源。

Method: 提出"音位条件分析"：在同一首歌内，将标记音位所在的音节与匹配的非标记对照音节进行比较，从而在保持歌手、旋律、体裁不变的条件下隔离音位的声学效应。覆盖九种类型学多样语言、数千首歌曲，沿五个声学维度测量效应，并基于歌曲层面效应剖面进行九分类语言识别（分折按艺术家分组）。

Result: 基于音位效应构建的歌曲级剖面在九分类任务中取得85.5%的平衡准确率（分折按艺术家分组）。可分离性是否源于音位局部效应本身的累积，论文未作定论。

Conclusion: 音系结构会在每种语言的歌唱方式中留下系统且可测量的痕迹，语言间声乐的可分辨性可由音位层面的声学效应刻画。

Abstract: The vocal music of each language carries a distinctive sonic identity, even without instrumental accompaniment. We ask whether these differences are measurable and traceable to specific phonemes. To tackle this question, we introduce phoneme-conditional analysis, which isolates the acoustic effect of typologically distinctive phonemes by comparing marker syllables against matched non-marker controls within the same song, holding singer, melody, and genre constant. Across nine typologically diverse languages and thousands of songs, we measure effects along five acoustic dimensions. Song-level profiles built from these effects identify the language of an unaccompanied vocal at 85.5% balanced accuracy in a nine-way classification with folds grouped by artist; whether the separability arises by accumulation of the phoneme-local effects themselves is left open. Our findings suggest that phonological structure leaves systematic and measurable traces in how each language is sung.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Influence of wall thermal boundary condition on mean-flow characteristics of a Mach 2.5 fully rough turbulent boundary layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09341)
*Mateus A. R. Braga,Robyn L. Macdonald*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过Mach 2.5下六个直接数值模拟，研究正弦粗糙壁（k+=79.1, ES=0.5）在三个壁温（Tw/Tr=1.0, 0.7, 0.4）下对湍流边界层动量与热输运的影响，揭示粗糙壁对雷诺类比、可压缩变换及热粗糙函数的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高超声速/可压缩湍流边界层中，粗糙壁与壁温耦合效应对摩阻与传热的影响尚未充分理解；现有可压缩变换与广义雷诺类比在粗糙壁和冷壁条件下的适用性存在争议，需要系统的高保真数据验证与修正。

Method: 对Mach 2.5平板湍流边界层进行六组直接数值模拟，匹配Reτ=784；壁面分别为光滑与三维正弦粗糙（有效斜率0.5, k+=79.1），壁温比Tw/Tr取1.0、0.7、0.4；分析平均壁面剪切与传热、应力分解、粗糙子层及壁面偏移，并检验半局部粗糙雷诺数匹配、可压缩变换、广义雷诺类比与热粗糙函数。

Result: 粗糙壁导致摩阻系数与传热系数增大，经典雷诺类比在粗糙壁失效；壁温间剪应力差异由黏性分量主导（尽管压力分量占优）。粗糙子层R_RSL=3k-6k，壁面偏移d=d_Θ≈0.5k（对应峰谷半高而非"平均粗糙高度"）。动量边界层在半局部粗糙雷诺数k*匹配下恢复不可压粗糙函数Δu1+，提出等效沙粒粗糙度ks*≈3.7k*；现有可压缩变换在任意壁温与粗糙度下均成立，反驳近期相反论断；广义雷诺类比对光滑壁成立、粗糙壁失败，提出并验证粗糙修正项；可压缩平均温度变换在冷壁尤其(Tw-Te)/(Tr-Te)<0时失效；热粗糙函数ΔΘ+因变换奇异性需谨慎报告。

Conclusion: 可压缩粗糙壁边界层的动量与热输运可通过半局部尺度实现与不可压结果的统一描述，并需引入粗糙度修正与等效沙粒粗糙度；广义雷诺类比与温度变换的失效（尤其冷壁）需明确界定适用范围。

Abstract: We investigate turbulent boundary layers at Mach 2.5 over sinusoidal roughness at matched $Re_τ=784$. We considered three wall temperatures, $T_w/T_r=$ 1.0, 0.7, and 0.4. Each wall temperature was repeated with a smooth and rough surface, the latter following a three-dimensional sinusoidal profile with effective slope 0.5 and matched $k^+=79.1$. In total six direct numerical simulations were completed. Analysis of mean wall shear and heat transfer detailed the augmentation in skin friction and heat transfer coefficient between smooth and rough cases; resulting in the classical failure of the Reynolds analogy for rough walls. We also show that differences in shear stress across wall temperatures are driven by the viscous component even though the pressure component dominates. The roughness sublayer was found to be $R_{RSL}=3k-6k$, consistent with the literature. Moreover, the wall offset $d=d_Θ\approx0.5k$ for both momentum and thermal boundary layers, signifying the offset represents the half peak-to-valley height rather than the "mean roughness height." For the mean momentum boundary layer, we find the present conditions recover the incompressible roughness function $Δu_1^+$ when matched via the semi-local roughness Reynolds number $k^*$. For this reason, an equivalent sand-grain roughness of $k_s^*\approx3.7k^*$ is proposed. The existing compressible transformations hold regardless of wall temperature or roughness - contradicting recent claims otherwise. The generalized Reynolds analogy works for smooth walls but fails for rough walls; a roughness correction term is proposed and validated. Compressible mean temperature transformations fail for cold walls, especially when $(T_w-T_e)/(T_r-T_e)<0$. The thermal roughness function, $ΔΘ^+$, is reported with caution given transformation singularities.

</details>


### [111] [Macroscale vortex impingement at a porous-fluid interface induces local heat-transfer enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09635)
*Thibaut K. Kemayo,Vishal Srikanth,Justin Courter,Rodrigo R. Caballero,Andrey V. Kuznetsov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过二维孔隙解析模拟，研究了方柱尾迹冲击多孔层时的涡输运与换热特性，揭示了宏观涡在界面处破碎、微观孔隙尺度波动在下游形成，以及冲击对界面换热的增强与衰减规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部生成的尾迹冲击多孔层在多种实际换热应用中普遍存在，但这些宏观尺度涡的输运过程及其对多孔层内换热的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 采用二维孔隙解析模拟，考察方柱钝体尾迹冲击由顺排加热方柱构成的多孔层，参数为Re=500-4000、Pr=0.7与7.0、孔隙率φ=0.75、0.85、0.95。

Result: 入射宏观涡从多孔-流体界面处即开始破碎；界面入口区以宏观湍流热输运为主，下游波动则与孔隙几何对齐并由孔隙喉道剪切、分离和反复的柱体尾迹在微观尺度生成。冲击使界面局部努塞尔数相对非冲击区升高，增强幅度随孔隙率、Re和Pr变化，在φ=0.85、Pr=7.0、Re=1000时峰值增强达18.2%。尾迹相关的宏观湍流热流贡献在所有研究参数下均在深入多孔层约3-4个单元胞的一致入口区内衰减。Re≥1000时，持续的尾迹动量亏损使换热对比转为负值，冲击区换热低于非冲击区。

Conclusion: 宏观尾迹涡对多孔层换热的影响局限于约3-4个单元胞的入口区，其冲击在低Re下增强界面换热，但在Re≥1000时因尾迹动量亏损导致冲击区换热反而低于非冲击区。

Abstract: Externally generated wakes can impinge on porous layers in several practical heat transfer applications. However, the transport of these macroscale vortices and their resulting influence on heat transfer in the porous layer remain unknown. In this paper, two-dimensional pore-resolved simulations are used to examine a square-bluff-body wake impinging on a porous layer composed of an in-line array of heated square obstacles at Re = 500-4000, Pr = 0.7 and 7.0, and porosities $φ$ = 0.75, 0.85, and 0.95. The incident macroscale vortices break down starting immediately at the porous-fluid interface. Following macroscale turbulent thermal transport in this entrance region, fluctuations farther downstream are aligned with the pore geometry and are generated at the microscale level by pore-throat shear, separation, and repeated obstacle wakes. Direct impact locally improves heat transfer at the porous-fluid interface by increasing the local Nusselt number relative to a non-impingement region. The interfacial heat-transfer enhancement varies with porosity, Reynolds number, and Prandtl number, with a peak enhancement of 18.2% observed for $φ$ = 0.85, Pr = 7.0, and Re = 1000. The wake-associated macroscale turbulent heat-flux contribution decays within a consistence entrance region extending approximately 3-4 unit cells into the porous layer across all investigated porosities, Reynolds numbers, and Prandtl numbers. For Re$\ge$1000, the persistent wake momentum deficit subsequently causes the heat-transfer contrast to become negative, resulting in lower heat transfer in the impingement region than in the non-impingement region.

</details>


### [112] [Stable, Compact, and Direct Ghost-Cell Reconstruction: A Non-Iterative Approach for Embedded-Boundary Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10165)
*Narsimha Reddy Rapaka,Pankaj Jagad,Yacine Addad,Mohamed Kamel Riahi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出一种用于笛卡尔网格浸入边界法的直接、解析、非迭代虚拟单元重构框架。通过解析表达式直接在嵌入边界上施加Dirichlet和Neumann条件，利用有向无环图的拓扑排序将虚拟单元分层重构，无需迭代、矩阵求逆或存储几何相关重构权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虚拟单元法（ghost-cell）通常依赖镜像点插值重构，需要矩阵求逆以及预计算和存储与几何相关的重构权重，迭代成本高且内存开销大。本文旨在消除这些环节，建立直接解析重构框架，同时保证重构稳定性并保持紧凑的笛卡尔模板。

Method: 1. 建立直接解析重构框架，解析表达式直接在嵌入边界施加Dirichlet/Neumann边界条件，免除镜像点重构、矩阵求逆和几何相关权重存储；2. 将相邻虚拟单元之间的依赖关系建模为有向无环图，通过拓扑排序将虚拟单元划分为依赖层级，实现逐层重构而无需迭代更新；3. 结合混合虚拟单元（HGC），其中心可位于嵌入边界两侧，满足此前针对标量平流推导的线性重构稳定性判据，同时保持最近邻紧凑笛卡尔模板；4. 以扩展模板经典虚拟单元格式（CGC_ES）作为保持稳定性的参考，区分重构稳定性与模板紧凑性的影响；5. 利用同一解析关系直接获得嵌入边界上的解值和空间梯度，从而无需单独表面重构或滤波即可计算压力力、壁面应力、阻力与升力。

Result: 对绕圆柱和翼型流动的模拟表明：线性判据对本文考虑的非线性不可压缩Navier-Stokes算例仍是重构稳定性的有效指标。经典虚拟单元重构在违反该判据时产生伪振荡，而CGC_ES与HGC保持稳定。HGC还能以紧凑的最近邻模板满足稳定性要求。

Conclusion: 所提出的解析非迭代虚拟单元重构框架在保持紧凑笛卡尔模板的同时实现了稳定重构，并可直接在嵌入边界上获得解与梯度用于力/应力计算，为笛卡尔网格浸入边界法提供了一种高效且稳定的方案。

Abstract: A direct, analytical, non-iterative ghost-cell reconstruction framework is developed for Cartesian-grid embedded-boundary methods. Analytical expressions impose Dirichlet and Neumann boundary conditions directly at the embedded boundary, eliminating intermediate image-point reconstruction, matrix inversion, and precomputation or storage of geometry-dependent reconstruction weights. For the Cartesian stencil considered, dependencies among neighboring ghost cells form a directed acyclic graph. A topological ordering partitions ghost cells into dependency levels, permitting level-by-level reconstruction without iterative updates. The formulation is combined with hybrid ghost cells (HGC), whose centers may lie on either side of the embedded boundary. This placement satisfies the linear reconstruction-stability criterion previously derived for scalar advection while retaining a compact nearest-neighbour Cartesian stencil. An extended-stencil classical ghost-cell formulation (CGC_ES) serves as a stability-preserving reference, distinguishing the effects of reconstruction stability and stencil compactness.
  The same analytical relations provide solution values and spatial gradients directly on the embedded boundary, enabling evaluation of pressure forces, wall stresses, drag, and lift without separate surface reconstruction or filtering. Simulations of flow past a circular cylinder and an airfoil show that the linear criterion remains a useful indicator of reconstruction stability for the nonlinear incompressible Navier-Stokes cases considered. Classical ghost-cell reconstruction develops spurious oscillations when the criterion is violated, whereas CGC_ES and HGC remain stable. HGC additionally satisfies the stability requirement with a compact nearest-neighbour stencil.

</details>


### [113] [A stochastic modeling framework to generate 2-D rough-wall high-Reynolds-number turbulent boundary layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10236)
*Roozbeh Ehsani,Michele Guala*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出并扩展了一种二维随机模型，用于生成高雷诺数粗糙壁湍流边界层中的瞬时阶跃速度剖面，仅需少量关键流动参数即可再现实验统计特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气表面层流动因表面粗糙度和高雷诺数导致近壁区需要极高空间分辨率，计算难度大；现有模型往往依赖均匀动量区（UMZ）数据集，难以在缺乏此类数据的情况下复现高雷诺数粗糙壁湍流边界层。

Method: 将先前用于生成瞬时阶跃速度剖面的二维随机模型（包含UMZ、剪切层和涡旋）扩展至对数层顶部；仅使用Taylor微尺度λT、边界层高度δ、摩擦速度uτ和空气动力学粗糙度长度z0等少量关键参数；将随机模型与UMZ及涡旋特征的缩放分布进行整合。

Result: 模型再现了Saddoughi和Veeravalli（2000）测量的高雷诺数粗糙壁湍流边界层；所得统计矩、能谱和结构函数与实验结果进行了对比验证。

Conclusion: 该随机建模框架无需UMZ数据集即可有效复现高雷诺数粗糙壁湍流边界层，验证后的代码已在GitHub仓库公开。

Abstract: Atmospheric surface layer flows are computationally challenging, predominantly due to surface roughness and the high Reynolds number, both of which demand exceptionally high spatial resolution in the near-surface region. We have developed a 2-D stochastic-based model for the generation and the streamwise concatenation of instantaneous, step-like velocity profiles featuring key elements of wall turbulent flows, i.e., uniform momentum zones (UMZ) and shear layers (Ehsani et al. 2024a,b), and vortices (Ehsani et al. 2026). The model is extended herein to the top of the logarithmic layer to reproduce the high-Reynolds-number, rough-wall, turbulent boundary layer measured by Saddoughi and Veeravalli(2000), without the support of a UMZ dataset, using only a handful of critical flow parameters: the Taylor microscale λT, the boundary layer height δ, the friction velocity uτ, and the aerodynamic roughness length z0. The primary challenge lies in the integration of the stochastic model with the scaled distributions of the UMZ and vortex characteristics. The resulting statistical moments, energy spectra, and structure functions are compared against the experimental results. The validated code is made available in a GitHub repository.

</details>


### [114] [The effects of surfactant solubility on inertial Marangoni flow: theory and numerics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10337)
*Jun Eshima,Luc Deike,Howard A. Stone*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文在Eshima等(2026)实验与理论工作的基础上，理论并结合数值模拟系统研究了可溶性表面活性剂在气-液-气薄片界面上的诱导流动，建立了相似解的推导与分析框架，并刻画了可溶、不溶与无限可溶三种极限情形之间的映射与过渡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面活性剂是气-液界面常见污染物，其浓度梯度会引发表面张力梯度并驱动流动。局部沉积于洁净界面的表面活性剂会产生向外铺展流。Eshima等(2026)通过晚期相似解发现表面活性剂溶解度会增强该流动，且可溶性表面活性剂溶液可映射为等价的不溶性表面活性剂溶液，但缺乏系统的理论框架。

Method: 在Eshima等工作基础上进行理论与数值扩展：定量定义相关无量纲参数，构建可跨越宽广物理可达参数空间的相似解推导与分析框架，并将有限可溶性表面活性剂的解与不溶性、无限可溶性极限情形的解进行联系与对比。

Result: 理论定义了关键无量纲参数，其框架与数值模拟可覆盖广阔的物理可达参数空间；发现无限可溶极限的解表现为中间解，随着溶解度增大其持续时间不断延长，随后才达到最终与不溶性表面活性剂相映射的晚期解，即无限可溶极限的映射仅在瞬态成立。

Conclusion: 建立了分析表面活性剂诱导气-液-气薄片流动的通用理论框架，明确了有限溶解度解在瞬态上趋向无限可溶极限解、在最终晚期趋向可映射为不溶性表面活性剂解的层级结构，揭示了溶解度对流动演化的调控机制。

Abstract: Surface active agents (surfactants) are common contaminants found on most air-liquid interfaces. Surfactants lower the surface tension, and hence surfactant concentration gradients generate surface tension gradients, which leads to flow. A canonical surfactant-induced flow is the outward spreading due to the localised deposition of surfactants onto an otherwise clean liquid interface. Eshima et al. (Phys. Rev. Lett., accepted, 2026) demonstrated experimentally and theoretically, through a late-time similarity solution, that surfactant solubility enhances surfactant-induced flows of air-liquid-air sheets and that soluble surfactant solutions can be mapped onto equivalent insoluble surfactant solutions. Here we extend the work theoretically and numerically, developing a systematic framework for the derivation and analysis of such solutions. The relevant nondimensional parameters are quantitatively defined, and the physically accessible parameter space is wide, which our theory and numerical simulations are able to span. We link the solutions for finitely soluble surfactants to the solutions for the limiting regimes of insoluble and infinitely soluble surfactants, where the latter link only holds transiently: the solution of the infinitely soluble limit appears as an intermediate solution that persists ever longer as solubility increases, before the ultimate late-time solution, which maps onto insoluble surfactants, is obtained.

</details>


### [115] [Dynamic Reduced-Order Data Assimilation from Sparse Velocity Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10497)
*Mauricio Portilla,Felipe Galarce,Ernesto Castillo,Benjamin Herrmann*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出降阶动力学同化（RODAS）框架，融合稀疏测量与参数化降阶模型，单步推断整条流场演化轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏速度测量重建高分辨率、时间解析的流场是实验流体力学难题；传统降阶数据同化需估计独立快照或将时间作为额外参数，难以同时处理空间外推、时间超分辨与参数变异性。

Method: 先用DMD从稀疏测量中识别主导动力学，再将识别出的动力学投影到由高保真数值模拟生成的参数化POD流形上，从而以单次推断重建整条全阶动力学轨迹，并自然纳入参数变异性。

Result: 在圆柱绕流涡脱落（牛顿流体与Carreau-Yasuda非牛顿流体）上验证：可从局部低分辨率测量准确重建高分辨率速度场，降阶基足够丰富时误差低于1%，在严重时间欠采样下仍稳健，并能准确预测阻力系数等工程关注量。

Conclusion: RODAS为从稀疏实验数据实时、物理信息驱动的非定常流场重建提供了高效框架。

Abstract: We present a novel reduced-order data assimilation framework, termed Reduced-Order Dynamical Assimilation (RODAS), for reconstructing high-resolution, time-resolved flow fields from sparse velocity measurements. The method combines low-dimensional experimental observations with a physics-based parametric reduced-order model, enabling both spatial extrapolation beyond the measurement region and temporal super-resolution. The approach first identifies the dominant dynamics from sparse measurements through Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD), and subsequently reconstructs the corresponding full-order flow evolution by projecting the identified dynamics onto a parametric Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) manifold generated from high-fidelity numerical simulations. Unlike conventional reduced-order data assimilation methods that estimate independent snapshots or treat time as an additional parameter, RODAS reconstructs an entire dynamical trajectory in a single inference step while naturally incorporating parametric variability. We assess the proposed methodology on vortex shedding behind a circular cylinder for both Newtonian and non-Newtonian (Carreau-Yasuda) fluids. Numerical experiments demonstrate accurate reconstruction of high-resolution velocity fields from localized, low-resolution measurements, achieving sub-percent reconstruction errors with sufficiently rich reduced bases, robust performance under severe temporal undersampling, and accurate prediction of engineering quantities of interest such as the drag coefficient. These results demonstrate that RODAS provides an efficient framework for real-time, physics-informed reconstruction of unsteady flows from sparse experimental data.

</details>

### [116] [Rogue-like waves from collision of mKdV solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10512)
*Stephen C. Anco,Jaskaran Maa*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 在修正KdV方程中，一反一正两个孤立波相互作用可产生类畸形波（rogue-like wave）。渐近衰减情形下，相互作用峰值与最高入射波之比最大为2.41（速度近似相等时），速度比1.5时降为2；趋于非零常数背景时，该比值在速度比为6.32时达到最大2.65。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究修正Korteweg-de Vries（mKdV）方程中相反极性孤立波相互作用能否产生类畸形波，并量化其放大特性，弥补以往多关注同极或衰减孤立波相互作用的不足。

Method: 基于修正KdV方程，考察一反一正两个孤立波的相互作用；分别针对渐近衰减波与趋于非零常数背景波两类情形，通过解析或数值手段计算相互作用剖面峰值与最高入射波高度之比，并分析其随两波速度比的变化规律。

Result: 衰减波情形：最大比值为2.41，出现在两波速度近似相等时；当速度比增至1.5时该比值降至2。非零常数背景情形：最大比值为2.65，出现在速度比为6.32时。

Conclusion: mKdV方程中相反极性孤立波的相互作用可产生类畸形波，其放大倍数显著大于1（最高可达2.65），且强烈依赖两波速度比与背景条件，为类畸形波的形成提供了一种孤立波相互作用机制。

Abstract: Interactions of two solitary waves with an up and a down orientation in the modified Korteweg-de Vries equation are shown to produce rogue-like waves. For waves that asymptotically vanish, the maximum ratio between the height of the interaction profile and the height of the tallest incoming wave is 2.41 when the waves have approximately equal speeds, and this ratio decreases to 2 when the speed ratio is 1.5. For waves that approach a non-zero constant at infinity, the same ratio reaches a maximum of 2.65 when the speed ratio of the waves is 6.32.

</details>
